I hereby salute the General Assembly of our Veteran Assembly and our beloved nation. May the blessings and blessings of God be upon you as the first speaker. All the ministries and institutions of our government are important, but the Ministry of National Education is of particular importance. In fact, success in every field is passed by the Ministry of National Education, so it has the initiative to raise generations and build our civilisations. It is the responsibility of our generations to grow up to be decent, constructive individuals, critical, thinking, communication skills, entrepreneurs, bodies, and mental health. It also provides healthy data for national and international measuring reports, as its goals are measurable when everyone has access to education under equal and just conditions. In response to our emphasis on education, it can only be achieved in the short, medium, long-term, which, by nature, dates more than four, eight, twelve, sixteen, twenty years. With the EC PARTY governments since 2002, we have begun to receive national and international positive reports in the mid-term, such as six or seven years, under the law of training and transitioning to 4+4+4+4. According to the latest U.N. Human Development Report, our country rose to the 59th rank between 189 countries in the Human Development Index, which includes the level of human development, income, education and health indicators, and for the first time we entered the category of highest development. This ranking is not enough for our country, so we're going to keep going up the stairs. The level of human development for our country was central in 2002, peak in 2009, highest in 2019. Our readers over 15 years of age increased from 85 percent in 2002 to 96 percent in 2019. According to these reports, Turkey has increased average schooling time in each level of education. Their average education period was 5.5 years in the 2000s, while 2019 was 7.7 years in the school year and 16.4 years in the school year. Our buying power has contributed to these increases. Our purchase power, which was $6,974 in 2002, rose to 24.905 in 2019. Those with less than $2 per day income, with poverty limits, were 18 per cent of the population in 2002, and today the rate was zeroed. As it turns out, there have been significant developments in PISA, and I'm running out of time. So, how did you get this? Our governments have been giving the highest budget to the Ministry of National Education since 2002. At first, we raised all of these rates with conditional education support to get girls into education and free textbooks, from 13 million to 18 million students, up to 70,000, up 50 percent in our schools. We brought the latest technologies to our schools; we presented them to the use of our teachers, our students. From disabled students to vocational education, we have performed successful services in all areas. Our Minister Ziya Selçuk Bey and all the employees of the Ministry of Education, administrative, education, and auxiliary services, who were in the first administration of the presidential system, have contributed to this, and I wish them success. I'd like to thank all of our ministers for their services in the EC PARTY governments. We're grateful to whoever contributed to the training. We also thank our President Recep Tayyip Erdogan, who led us to every success in our country, for his contribution to education. Today, this has not changed, and with 177 billion 605 million, 504,000 liras, 16.2 per cent of the budget is earmarked for education. Mr. President, the esteemed MPs have begun to receive feedback from our country's investments in national education. For example, young people who were high school and high school students in the early years of the AK PARTI rule today serve in many strategic areas, particularly the defence industry and the energy sector. THE MILGE Project, ALTAY tank, national infantry rifle, and in particular, some of our oil exploration activities in the eastern Mediterranean are just a few of them. Our ministry has done important work on many issues that are essential to the future of national education, and has shared with the public the 2023 Education Vision Document, which came as a result of these studies. Our most important priority in this area is to address the differences of quality and quantity between our schools, to ensure the equality of opportunity and opportunity, and to improve the physical conditions that our schools have. I think it would be helpful to share some data from the work done so far in the headlines I've been talking about. Since 2003, 315,884 has been introduced to the new class service so far, and the total number of classrooms we have was 578,338. The increase is up to 83 per cent in 17 years. Of course, in the process, we did not neglect to increase our qualifications as we increased the number of classrooms. We have equipped our classrooms with high-end materials such as education materials, smart boards, courses, and lab equipment that support teaching programs. The most important innovation to ensure the equality of opportunities in education during our rule is the application of free distribution of textbooks to our students. In this context, from 2003 to 2019, we distributed a total of 3 billion, 3 million,771,000 books to our students for free. Within the budget of 2020, along with the President's budget for this application, the total amount of 1 billion 334 million Turkish liras envisioned to allocate Social Aid and Solidarity from the Support Fund. One thing I'd like to open a separate title here is a matter of private education. Our ministry gives great importance to the physical, mental, and professional development of individuals in need of special education. Through integration, training practices, special education classes, private education schools, and training opportunities at home or at the hospital are provided under the control of our Ministry and are allowed to integrate with social life by taking advantage of the equal educational opportunities of individuals in need of special education. The other thing our ministry is sensitive about is vocational education. The way our business world needs a qualified labour force is by improving the quality of vocational education. In order to strengthen education and development relations, patents, good models and design have been targeted at all vocational and technical secondary institutions, and initiatives in this direction have begun to be supported by the Ministry. In this context, the theme of the 2019-2020 school year is defined as "patent, useful model and design." Mr. President, valuable lawmakers, examples of investments and work we've made in the field of education can of course be replicated. But what's really important is what we're going to do about our future and how we imagine 2023, 2053, 2071 Turkey. AK PARTY has never been a party of daily policies and short-term accounts since it was founded. As in other areas, the goal of all our studies in the field is to leave a strong Turkey legacy to future generations, which is firm on the ground.
